# Test-suite inventory (2026-09-16, #4235)

Generated by `dart run tool/test_inventory.dart`. One row per test file — **1754 files, 16857 cases**.

The classification is a static scan. **KEEP** means no determinism risk was detected, not that every case was re-read. **REPLACE** names a file that proves the TEXT and nothing else (a source scan with zero executed assertions); a residual scan beside real behaviour coverage is **KEEP+REFACTOR** instead — the scan should go, but the file is not the #4116 false-green risk. **KEEP+REFACTOR** also covers real sleeps, wall-clock reads, goldens and flaky tags. Duplicate detection is manual and recorded as it happens.

- **Actions:** KEEP **1487** · KEEP+REFACTOR **267**
- **Layers:** other **502** · widget **488** · data **330** · provider **179** · domain **169** · architecture-lint **73** · tooling **6** · integration **4** · l10n **3**
- **Risk flags:** wall-clock **141** · real-sleep **129** · source-scan **71** · network-tagged **10** · flaky-tagged **2** · golden **1**
